金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 19 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 9 月通膨升溫，Fed 年底前再升息機率仍高： 美國 9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，高於 8 月的
3.4%；核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.4%升至 2.6%，顯示通膨壓力不只來自能源，核心價格亦有回
升。隔日的 PPI 年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%，反映上游成本壓力仍強。通膨重新升溫，使Fed
即使面對就業降溫，也難以快速轉向寬鬆，市場對年底前再度升息的預期仍高。
◼ 台積電財報創新高，AI 需求強勁但市場轉向檢視融資風險：台積電 10/15 公布其第三季淨利

達 7,631 億元、年增 69%，連續第六季刷新紀錄，第三季美元營收 467.2 億美元，並預估第
四季營收將升至 523～543 億美元 ； 公司同時將2026 全年營收成長預估提高至約 44%， 反映
AI 晶片與先進製程需求仍極為強勁。然而，高利率環境使市場開始檢視 AI 資料中心業者的槓
桿與融資能力，CoreWeave 約 500 億美元債務引發信用疑慮，引發市場波動。
◼ 中東衝突再度推高能源風險 ， 油價高檔加劇全球通膨壓力 ：胡塞武裝上週攻擊沙烏地阿美設施 ，
伊朗亦表示採取新的海上攻擊策略，使荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣航運風險再度升高。10/16 布蘭特
原油與西德洲原油期貨雙雙收在近三週高點。
市場展望
◼ 科技股中期趨勢料將不變 ， 但短線波動勢將加劇 ：近期市場同時面臨通膨升溫 、AI 資本支出疑
慮與重要科技財報公佈在即等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能受到壓抑。在利率維持高檔及市
場評價偏高的環境下，市場短線料將隨財報結果與市場預期而出現較大波動。然而，上週台積
電與 ASML 財報結果整體優於預期 ， 可見AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡象 ，

科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，惟後續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出變動及科技子
產業的表現分化現象。
◼ 中天期美債與投資級債債券兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性 ， 加上政府與企業發債供
給維持高檔，可能使市場利率持續處於較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企
業獲利、現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，加上較高的美國公債基準利率，使投資級債的利息收益
仍具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較容易
受到公債供給增加 、 期限溢酬及利率波動影響 ， 因此中天期美國公債與投資債券較能兼顧息收
與利率風險。

## Document 085: 261012_ml_nytech

Source PDF: all report pdf/261012_ml_nytech.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 21
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Nanya Technology

Upbeat 3Q margin/ASP, and 2027 guidance 
also optimistic: lift PO to NT$800

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 800.00 TWD | Price: 527.00 TWD

3Q results confirm super-cycle even with legacy DRAM 
Nanya Tech, a pure DRAM supplier, reported very strong 3Q results (sales: NT$134bn, 
+62% QoQ / +612% YoY; OPM: 80% - similar to big-3 DRAM makers’; net margin: 66%). 
The key contributor should be upbeat ASP (up 60%+ QoQ vs consensus of 30-40%). In 
fact, Nanya Tech had already reported more than doubled 1H ASP (60%+ QoQ for two 
consecutive quarters). Thus, Street expected limited QoQ price increase in 3Q (vs 1H).  
According to Nanya Tech President Pei Ing Lee, demand for legacy DRAM products 
(DDR3 & DDR4) remained strong across IT, industrial, and enterprise applications. Mgmt 
also said legacy DRAM accounted for 80%+ of 3Q sales (vs 10% range for DDR5). Net-
net, the 3Q results confirmed super-cycle even selling mainly legacy/conventional DRAM. 
Guidance broadly bullish; no sign of a price cut 
Lee provided bullish guidance (earnings call). What we learned: (1) rising 4Q ASP, (2) 
ongoing shortage of legacy DRAM through 2027 (no immediate demand shift from 
DDR4 to DDR5), (3) new fab mass production (2027-28), (4) significant FCF generation 
(with high margin profile) despite large capex spend (NT$70bn in 2026), (5) record-high 
net cash (NT$270bn in 3Q), (6) new node development (in-house) and migration (1c, 1d, 
1e) on track, and (7) new construction plan for 3D wafer packaging/testing facilities in 
Ping Tung Science Park, Taiwan. Overall, new guidance presents further growth (capacity 
expansion) and high margin profile (favorable ASP trend). 
Lift PO to NT$800 on revisions to 2027/28 EPS estimates 
We lift our PO from NT$750 to NT$800 using the same target P/E vs earlier (9x 2027-
28E EPS). Thus, our PO revision is mostly based on 7-8% revisions to 2027-28 EPS 
estimates. We assume 5%/1ppt higher sales/OPM vs our earlier forecasts given more 
favorable conventional DRAM ASP trend. To be conservative, we assume negative EPS 
growth for 2028 but it can turn positive if the ASP remains stable. We reiterate Buy.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Nanya Technology is an affiliate of Formosa Plastics Group. 
The firm manufactures and distributes DRAM chips. 
Management focuses on non-PC applications such as 
consumer, mobile, server, autos, etc. Key customers are 
based in China and Taiwan. Samsung, SK Hynix and Micron 
are major competitors.

Investment Rationale 
Our Buy rating is based on the DRAM industry's supercycle, 
which should warrant a high-margin profile for Nanya Tech 
even in the long term. Its 3Q26 results proved record-high 
revenue (NT$134bn, more than 5x higher than the previous 
peak cycle) and 80% operating margin. Management focus 
on long-term contracts, including DDR4 for eSSD or servers, 
should also act as a catalyst. Significantly rising net cash 
even after capex hike could also support higher dividend 
payouts.

Stock Data  
Price to Book Value 3.7x

BofA Memory Indicator 
Exhibit 25: The BofA Memory Indicator still presents super-cycle – much higher than 2017-18 
peak level; of course, it slightly declined to 179 in Aug-26 (due to lower YoY growth) after 
hitting peak of 190 in Mar/Apr-26, driven by exceptionally strong DRAM/NAND spot pricing, 
rising ASPs and billings, along with Korea’s ~200%+ export growth. 
BofA Memory Indicator – back to upturn level since Oct-2025 and hit highest peak in Mar/Apr-26 
(back-tested)

Note: Our indicator had previously been capped at ~140, but the recent unprecedented surge—driven by memory spot prices, ASPs, and 
billings—has led us to raise the ceiling (up to 240 levels) to reflect a more accurate relative comparison. This exceptional strength is 
further validated by strong earnings from memory chipmakers. 
Source: DRAMeXchange, WSTS, MoTIR Korea, BofA Global Research

fund. Back-tested performance depicts the theoretical (not actual) performance of a particular strategy over the time period indicated. 
No representation is being made that any actual portfolio is likely to have achieved returns similar to those shown herein.

performance results are created by applying an investment strategy or methodology to
historical data and attempts to give an indication as to how a strategy might have
performed during a certain period in the past if the product had been in existence during
such time. Back-tested results have inherent limitations including the fact that they are
calculated with the full benefit of hindsight, which allows the security selection
methodology to be adjusted to maximize the returns. Further, the results shown do not

reflect actual trading or the impact that material economic and market factors might
have had on a portfolio manager's decision-making under actual circumstances. Back-
tested returns do not reflect advisory fees, trading costs, or other fees or expenses.

### Page 16

Price objective basis & risk 
Nanya Technology (NNYAF) 
Our PO of NT$800 is derived from 9x 2027-28E P/E, which is c.30% below the long-
term historical average (13x during 2014-25), but near-50% above the previous upturn 
period (6x in 2014-18). Memory stocks usually trade at low valuation multiples during 
peak cycle due to downturn concerns. However, the current DRAM cycle looks 
structurally much better than ever and we expect 2027-28 earnings to exceed 2026 level 
due to the AI growth theme. This could lead to a re-rating (e.g., 9x P/E or higher), with 
solid earnings outlook.

Upside risks: DRAM price increases even after the 1H26 upturn, Big-3 memory 
chipmakers' significant supply cut for commodity DRAM due to HBM, and Nanya Tech's 
more successful ramp-up of DDR4 or DDR5 (better cost or margin improvement).

Downside risks: weaker legacy DDR4 demand (quicker shift to DDR5), and China's more 
successful memory production increase.

potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings ar e: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things,

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
CPO: Optical scale-up is getting closer to reality. A 64-XPU CPO-enabled
compute-rack demonstration highlighted the potential for direct optical links

between accelerators. As 200G/lane electrical channels approach physical
limitations, optical scale-up will become more important, particularly for customized
XPUs. However, pluggagle optics should retain scale-out volume leadership given
advantages in serviceability and cost. Packaging yield, fiber alignment, and reliability
remain key commercialization challenges.
Advanced packaging: Moving beyond 2.5D. MediaTek highlighted custom ASIC
designs approaching 10x reticle size, arguing that 3.5D integration could deliver

approximately 10x better die-to-die energy efficiency versus conventional 2.5D
interconnects. Management expects increasing adoption over the next 3-5 years.
MediaTek also emphasized custom HBM, proprietary die-to-die interconnects, and
DTCO optimization. We see structural opportunities for advanced packaging and
custom ASIC design services as system complexity increases (link).
Memory: Capacity matters alongside bandwidth. SK hynix introduced its MARS

disaggregated memory architecture, demonstrating a 3-5x improvement in time-to-
first-token through shared KV-cache access versus recomputation. Meanwhile, HBF
targets approximately half of HBM bandwidth but 14x higher capacity, with samples
expected in early 2028. Inference workloads will increasingly require heterogeneous
memory architectures encompassing HBM, DRAM, and NAND, potentially expanding
memory content beyond traditional GPU-attached HBM (link).

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Optical interconnect: CPO is moving toward rack-scale scale-up, while pluggables
retain a scale-out advantage.
Custom ASICs and packaging: MediaTek sees 3.5D gaining traction as 2.5D
approaches physical limits
Memory architecture: SK hynix targets inference capacity and KV-cache
bottlenecks through MARS arid HBF.
Power and cooling: Racks above 200kW accelerate 800V DC, vertical power

delivery and liquid cooling.

Power and thermal: The next system-level constraints. Demonstrations of 6kw-
class double-sided cold plates vertical power delivery, and advanced microchannel
cooling highlighted intensifying thermal challenges. The transition toward 800V DC
should reduce conversion losses and improve power density. Meanwhile, AMD's
Helion agentic Al rack architecture reinforces incremental CPU demand alongside
GPU infrastructure. Power delivery, advanced cooling, and system integration

capabilities to become increasingly important differentiators.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O or Over) - The stock's total return is expected to exceed the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry
team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E or Equal) - The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or

industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR) - Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's
industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U or Under) - The stock's total return is expected to be below the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry

team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 113: 261015_ml_MTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/261015_ml_MTK.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

MediaTek

ASIC business expanding into Bytedance; 
potential high-end mobile gains in ‘27

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 6,200 TWD | Price: 4,475 TWD

Bytedance expanding ASIC TAM beyond Google/xAI 
Bloomberg reported on October 15th that Bytedance is close to an agreement to work 
with Mediatek on the ASIC design and turnkey service support following its collaboration 
with Broadcom and Qualcomm. This is consistent with our update in September that 
Mediatek could co-develop an AI inference chipset with Bytedance from 1H27 for tape 
out in 1H28 and see contribution from production in early ’29. Similar to Mediatek and 
xAI’s collaboration, we believe Bytedance would need the support on sourcing 
components (e.g. HBM and substrate) in addition to wafer manufacturing/packaging, 
lifting the dollar content vs. Google’s TPUs. Assuming 1mn unit demand annually & 
US$20-25k ASPs including advanced logic dies and HBM, the project could contribute 
US$20-25bn to Mediatek’s sales in ’29 (vs. BofA sales expectation on Mediatek  of 
US$82bn in ’28 with US$45bn from Google TPUv8/v9 and US$9bn from xAI’s 1-2 
quarter contribution). 
A modest tailwind in mobile as it gains share at Samsung 
The mobile chipset builds from Mediatek and Qualcomm in 2H26 are tracking below 
seasonal in 2H26 as the end demand is impacted by the elevated memory cost. While 
the market outlook looms into ’27 (vs. -25% Android unit demand in ’26), we expect 
Mediatek’s mobile business to fare better especially in the high-end segment where it 
could gain unit share by penetrating into Samsung’s flagship smartphones historically 
dominated by Qualcomm and Samsung’s own SoCs. Factoring 30% share in Samsung’s 
high-end mobile shipment of 30-35mn annual units and US$200+ chipset ASPs, it could 
be ~20% accretive to Mediatek’s mobile sales and ~5% of its total business from 2H27. 
EPS and valuation downside limited after recent pullback 
We maintain our Buy rating & NT$6,200 PO on 24x 2H27-1H28E P/E (implied 30x ASIC 
EPS + 10x merchant EPS). The share has been dragged by TPU competition concern in 
the near term but could be lifted by expanding ASIC projects and high-end mobile gains.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$6,200 is based on 24x 2H27-1H28 P/E. which is towards the higher end 
of its historical P/E range of 8-25x, as we believe the company's broadening ASIC 
pipeline could drive an earnings upgrade cycle.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment rating s are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a

本次報告：法說會

系統電 (5309 TT) 
2027 年重回成長軌道

永豐觀點 
系統電連三個月營收創下新高，預期在 BBU 及無人載具開始出貨下，營收逐月成
長。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持中立投資建議：系統電在經歷營運轉型整合期後，2027 年營收及獲利將重返
成長 ， 然研究員仍維持中立的投資建議 ， 主係考量(1) 營運槓桿效應須待 2H27 時，
BBU 放量出貨較明顯。此外，系統電為因應未來產能持續擴充 BBU 及無人機產線
侵蝕獲利；(2) 評價上，以 2027 年稀釋後稅後 EPS 2.17 元計算，目前本益比約
30 倍，評價尚屬合理，故維持中立的投資建議，持續關注 BBU 出貨及新訂單狀
況。

### Page 2

2

系統電營運主力將轉移至 BBU 相關應用： 系統電子工業股份有限公司成立於1977
年，原專注在車用電子、能源、通訊、電玩遊戲。近年公司陸續進行業務調整及轉
型 ， 將業務分為汽車電子 、 工業物聯網 、AI 資料中心能源系統管理三大事業 ，2015
年推出藍牙胎壓偵測器(BLE TPMS)， 目前主要經營業務內容為車用胎壓偵測系統 、
車用電子產品、綠色能源及儲能設備、電源供應器，並獲有多項專利，並持續擴大
BLE TPMS 應用在歐美 OE 前裝市場，如新能源訂動車、大客車車隊、皮卡等。近
期受到資料中心用電需求增加，系統電 BBU 相關產品將開始出貨。1H26 產品比
重：TPMS 46%、工業電腦 38%、AI 資料中心能源系統及其他 16%。 
3Q26 強固型工業電腦出貨旺，：系統電 9 月營收 3.67 億元(+12.04%MoM，
+17.87%YoY)，連續三個月創下歷史新高，主係因強固型電腦產品出貨。雖 BBU
及無人機相關產品尚未大量出貨，然在工業電腦領域，受惠產業趨勢帶動，如農耕
車及無人載具相關應用成長快速，使系統電 3Q26 營收 10.14 億元(+13%QoQ，
+24%YoY)，研究處預估毛利率 23.2%(+1ppt QoQ，+1.9ppt YoY)，因積極投入
研發及新產品設計使營業費用較高，稅後淨利 0.04 億元，稅後 EPS 為 0.02 元。 
4Q26 兩大專案 BBU 及無人機小量出貨 ：先前耕於已久的 BBU 產品將於十月開始
出貨 ， 初期以3kW 產品為主 ， 系統電已完成建置 2 條全自動 與 1 條半自動 BBU
產線。無人機方面，包含軍用及微型軍警偵察需求強勁，產線亦完成建置。研究處
預期 4Q26 營收 11.17 億元(+10.2%QoQ，+28%YoY)，毛利率 24%(+0.8ppt 
QoQ，-0.6ppt YoY)，稅後淨利 0.07 億元，稅後 EPS 為 0.03 元。預估 2026 年營
收 38.9 億元(+14.7%YoY)，毛利率 23.6%(+0.1pts YoY) ，稅後淨利 0.24 億元(-
54.3%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 0.11 元。 
2027 年迎來營運收成期：展望 2027 年，以三大事業體區分，工業電腦方面將聚
焦於邊緣算力模組、GCS 地面控制站，部門產品毛利率最佳，有利於獲利表現；AI
資料中心能源系統方面，BBU 產品將開始大量出貨，並持續擴充產能，預估部門
營收將年增 328%，並將增加 3-4 條 BBU 產線，因應未來新產品的加入；TPMS
業務收到車市影響，營運相對平淡。綜上所述，系統電2027 年營運將開始收成過
去在無人載具及 BBU 電源產品的投入，研究處預期 2027 年營收 61.68 億元
(+58.5%YoY)，毛利率 24.9%(+1.3pts YoY) ，稅後淨利 5.41 億元，稅後 EPS 為
2.36 元。

研 究  報 告                
樺漢(6414.TT) 
產業：電子–電腦及週邊設備
3Q26 營收季增 3%，4Q26 營收預估與 3Q26 相當 
 投資評價與建議 
未來營收成⻑端視北美建廠需求與AI ， 毛利率提升主要仰賴整併Kontron的綜
效：樺漢計畫拿下3億美元之實體AI市佔，工控物聯網成⻑將由邊緣AI帶動，
北美建廠需求使智慧工廠持續成⻑，毛利率則藉由產品組合調整、新服務模式
的滲透率與整併Kontron的綜效以向上提升。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26營收季增3%(年增48%) ， 智慧工廠3Q26營收季減2%(年增84%) ：3Q26
營收496.4億元季增3%(年增48%)符合預估值(496.5億元)，工業物聯網事業
群、智慧軟體與方案事業群及智慧工廠與廠務事業群，分別季增4%、9%及-
2%(年增44%、-9%及84%)，智慧軟體與方案事業群4Q26為政府及企業單位
結帳高峰，4Q26營收比重可望高於3Q26，有助提升毛利率。 
4Q26營收預估與3Q26相當，在手訂單達2,550億元以上： 4Q26營收預估與
3Q26相當，成⻑驅動主要來⾃AI應用擴展 （由邊緣運算走向智慧平台）、半導
體擴產（先進製程/封裝/AI基建） 、智慧製造升級（⾃動化/機器人/數位轉型）
及全球在地化供應鏈布局；2,550億元以上的在手訂單能見度已延伸至 2027
年，其中工控物聯網受惠邊緣AI與交通/網絡設備強勁需求，智慧廠務工程持
續獲益於台灣及全球半導體大廠擴產與高科技廠務需求 ， 智慧軟體與方案則提
供高毛利且穩定的經常性收入。 
 投資風險 
整併效益不彰、實體AI需求不如預期。

與研發以開發替代方案，確保成本可控並維持毛利水準。 
樺漢三大事業群

資料來源 : 樺漢法說會資料，玉山投顧整理、繪製

### Page 3

3 
且穩定的經常性收入。 
樺漢看好實體AI：過去幾年全球聚焦於生成式AI，讓AI進入大眾生活與文字對話，樺漢認為生成式AI僅是起點，下⼀波
產業革命的關鍵在於實體AI，將讓AI開始進入真實世界，具備『看得見、聽得懂、想得通、做得到』的感知、理解、決
策與⾃主行動能力；根據Next Move Strategy Consulting預估，全球實體AI市場規模將由2025年的58 億美元成⻑至
2035年的1,200 億美元(CAGR35%)，10年預估成⻑將超過20倍；目前樺漢已全球部署超過100萬套系統，包含
Casino/Gaming (7.5萬/21萬套)、Lottery (6萬套)、ATM (25萬套)及POS/Self-Checkout (35萬套)，實體節點分布極為
廣泛；樺漢已打造⼀套共通核⼼技術平台，涵蓋Compute Platform (SOM/Edge)、Sensor Suite (Camera/LiDAR/IMU)
及Drive & Actuator、Connectivity (5G/Wi-Fi/TSN)，同⼀套平台可靈活⽀援 AMR（⾃主 移動機器人）、Mobile 
Manipulator （移動操作機器人）、Humanoid （人形機器人） 及Industrial Robot （工業機器人），大幅降低研發成本，
加速產線規模化複製落地 ； 樺漢機器人團隊已完成核⼼技術平台整合 。 早期 AMR 與工業⾃動化機器人已在智慧工廠與
物流場域大量量產；針對具備感知能力的人形機器人 （Humanoid）與 Mobile Manipulator，公司透過 NVIDIA Isaac
數位雙生進行驗證，預計於2026年起陸續展開客戶PoC與場域導入，並配合 Kontron 歐美通路擴大推廣，目標在10年
內拿下3億美元之實體AI市佔。 
樺漢與Kontron的整合策略定位

資料來源 : 樺漢法說會資料 
Kontron的EBITA/營收比率將由2026年的14%成⻑⾄2030年的16%：樺漢與Kontron之結合定位為 『One Platform， 
Two Strengths』，Kontron提供高階研發、KontronOS資安軟體與歐美品牌優勢，樺漢則提供全球規模化製造、供應
鏈採購與ODM執行能力，整合效益預期涵蓋四大構面 : 1、技術與產品整合，將KontronOS與5G/NAD/資安技術導入
樺漢硬體平台，拓展Edge AI / Physical AI方案，2、全球市場整合，實現亞洲、歐洲、北美區域交叉銷售與品牌聯合開
發，3、供應鏈整合，結合鴻海全球採購規模進行零組件採購優化，改善缺料及逾期積壓訂單，4、全球製造整合，執行
在地化製造策略，結合歐洲（捷克/保加利亞/匈牙利）、亞洲樺漢製造與北美區域生產；Kontron的2030年營收目標將
由2026年的16億歐元成⻑至2030年的26億歐元，EBITDA獲利目標將由2026年的2.25億歐元成⻑至2030年的4. 2億歐
元，EBITA/營收比率則將由 2026年的14% 成⻑至2030年的16%，軟 體解決方案收入將由 2026年的5.5億歐元倍增至 
2030年的10億歐元，核⼼成⻑動能包括鐵路通訊、國防航太及5G專網與關鍵連通技術；樺漢對Kontron持股已提高至
49.4% ， 總投資金額約2.87億歐元 ， 整合效應目標為EPS年貢獻2027年起達5元(獲利貢獻預估將佔稅前淨利的16%)以上 。

### Page 4

4 
樺漢與Kontron的整合策略定位

資料來源 : 樺漢法說會資料

| 樺漢的定位與優勢 
樺漢是鴻海艦隊的工業電腦旗艦：樺漢科技（6414）是鴻海集團旗下的重要工業電腦（IPC）與工業物聯網廠，是鴻海
艦隊的工業電腦旗艦。鴻海透過旗下投資公司共持有樺漢約24%的股權，為其最大股東與⺟集團，雙方在資源、供應鏈
及高科技AI智慧製造上緊密合作。樺漢的產品應用領域非常多元，包括智慧零售、智慧監控、智慧家居、資訊安全、交
通、製造、能源、醫療、建築、園區等，且透過整合硬體、軟體與人工智慧驅動服務積極推進ESaaS(樺漢解決方案即服
務)計畫，以滿足客戶多樣化需求。 
三大事業群架構分別為工業物聯網、智慧軟體與方案及智慧工廠與廠務：樺漢的三大事業群架構分別為工業物聯網事業
群、智慧軟體與方案事業群及智慧工廠與廠務事業群；工業物聯網事業群包括邊緣運算、網安通訊、工業與嵌入式系統
整合 。 智慧軟體與方案事業群著重高毛利軟硬雲整合 ， 涵蓋工業軟體 、 機器學習 、 數位雙生 、 雲地平台系統整合 ， Kontron 
在此板塊扮演關鍵的歐洲市場與軟體加值核⼼。智慧工廠與廠務事業群提供半導體設備及廠務系統、智能系統整合、高
科技材料與設備，由帆宣(6196)主導。 
併購帶來營收規模快速成⻑，三大事業群各有所⻑：樺漢透過併購壯大規模，營收由2015年的111億元僅短短十年就快
速10倍成⻑至2025年的1,423億元 ， 尤其入股Kontron(8/20後樺漢持股49.4%)與帆宣更是關鍵 。 樺漢看中Kontron的品
牌加持與歐洲的高市佔率，目前軌道交通產品Kontron已掌握歐洲逾50%市佔，並著手把相關方案導入亞太市場。樺漢
和Kontron在技術合作方面，建立共享平台，全面整合研發、製造、供應鏈、IT和人才等資源，協同設計產品，建構具
有全球競爭力的製造系統；產品整合則共享全球品牌資源，提升市場認知度與價值定位，並深入推廣產品線至亞太、歐
洲、北美等全球市場中⼼；市場聚焦方面，結合Kontron在基礎作業系統(Kontron OS)、物聯網軟體和5G網通方面的軟

### Page 5

5 
實力，以及樺漢在邊緣AI伺服器、機器人和強固型工業電腦方面的硬體優勢，共同打造實體AI技術平台。 
樺漢併購帶來營收規模快速成⻑

資料來源 : CMoney，玉山投顧整理、繪製 
實體 AI 帶來新⼀輪商機，樺漢拚五年內全球工業電腦第⼀名：實體 AI 的應用廣泛，如⾃動搬運叉車可應用於倉儲及
工廠內物料搬運，具備即時導航與避障，可提升效率並降低人力依賴。服務型機器人則可應用於清潔、導引及巡檢，
具有穩定感知與定位，可適用商辦、醫院、公共空間。樺漢認為，生成式 AI 只是開始。下⼀個十年真正改變世界的，
不只是 AI 會回答問題，而是 AI 開始進入真實世界。逐漸開始看得見、聽得懂、想得通、做得到。實體 AI 的定義應該
是讓 AI 具備感知、理解、決策與行動能力。樺漢預估 2035 年的實體 AI 市場規模可達 1,200 億美元，樺漢欲藉此商
機於五年內攻佔全球工業電腦第⼀名。樺漢目前已透過旗下全資⼦公司超恩，強化邊緣運算產品線，並結合 NVIDIA
的 Nemotron 模型，處理影像、聲音與文字等多模態資料。這類架構把資料分析留在終端端點執行，對應即時反應與
隱私要求較高的場域，也減少雲端端 AI 運算負擔。樺漢同步延伸 ESaaS(Ennoconn Solution as a Service，樺漢即服
務)服務內容，將硬體、軟體與人工智慧驅動服務整合到同⼀套方案中。樺漢 AI 聚焦在應用層與電力能源層，應用在智
慧製造、智慧城市、ESG 以及發展利基小語言模型。 
樺漢AI聚焦在應用層與電力能源層

資料來源 : 樺漢法說會資料  
對 Kontron 持股將提高⾄ 49.4%，5 年內計畫 ROE 由 12%提升⾄ 20%：2027 年樺漢持續轉型 ESaaS 軟硬整合與
訂閱制，期望使⻑期毛利率目標躍升至 25%以上，期望透過提升營業利益率、強化資產周轉率與管理資本結構，五年
內計畫將 ROE 由 12%提升至 20%，並提供股東 30％年化總股東報酬率（TSR）。高毛利智慧軟體與方案將帶動整體獲

6 
利體質持續改善，智慧工廠與廠務受惠於半導體廠務需求推動高科技設備與⾃動化系統營收成⻑，工業物聯網業務則
持續由零售與金融智慧化需求推動營收增⻑。未來三年營收目標呈現年增 10％、15％、25％的階梯式成⻑。Kontron
整合綜效目標是優化採購成本 1%至 5%，在產品組合質變下持續轉型，對營運將有加分。尤其是樺漢與 Kontron 整合
後將持續深耕軌道交通、航太國防、Kontron OS 以及邊緣運算平台等高成⻑市場，對未來毛利率的提升將有助益。樺
漢已取得德國外國直接投資審查核准，8/20 交割，樺漢對 Kontron 持股提高至 49.4%，總投資金額約 2.87 億歐元，
整合效應目標為 EPS 年貢獻 2027 年起達 5 元(獲利貢獻預估將佔稅前淨利的 16%)以上，與 Kontron 將從「收購預
期」走向實際控制力提升，後續重點將轉向共同採購、產品整合、軟體訂閱收入。

Kontron 的綜效而定。

| 工業電腦產業簡介 
工業電腦對環境的適應力高於個人電腦：1984年，IBM發表了5531型工業電腦，隨後於1985年推出基於Intel 80286處
理器的IBM7531，正式開啟工業電腦新紀元。工業電腦（IPC）發展至今已有40多年的歷史，從最初只負責簡單的流程
監測，進化到現今具備高效能算力的邊緣控制中⼼，其最大的特⾊在於使用「工業級用料」，能夠保障⻑達10至 15年
的使用需求，並且能克服各式嚴苛的環境挑戰，確保設備能持續穩定地運作。工業電腦最顯著的特性便是其針對惡劣環
境的耐用性，為了應對容易有大量粉塵的環境，多數的機型都採用「無風扇」和「⼀體成形」的設計，避免風扇故障引
起的熱當機或灰塵吸入造成的短路；此外，為因應戶外作業或極端氣候，大部分的工業電腦都會通過寬溫(例如：可運行
的溫度區間為 -40°C～85°C)與寬壓(例如：可輸入電壓區間為 9V～36V)測試，並搭載防塵、防水、防震、耐衝擊的機
制，確保設備在震動頻繁的環境下仍能穩定運作，工業電腦要具備嚴苛環境的適應力，高可靠度，擁有豐富的工業接口
及較⻑的生命週期管理。 
工業電腦與⼀般電腦的差異

資料來源 : Google、 玉山投顧整理、編製

### Page 7

7 
工作溫度 、 防護等級等為評定工業電腦優劣的重要指標 ， 以冷卻機制來說 ， 個人電腦依賴主動散熱 ， 灰塵與故障風險高 ，
工業電腦則採被動、密封設計，降低⻑期失效機率；擴充與客製化方面，工業電腦可高度客製，對應工業、醫療、車載
等垂直場域需求，而非僅僅只有通用規格；電源管理能力方面，工業電腦具備抗突波與點火控制，能在不穩定供電下持
續運作；至於作業系統穩定性方面，工業電腦多採用⻑期⽀援版本，可避免更新中斷營運。 
工業電腦優劣的重要指標

資料來源 : Google、 玉山投顧整理、編製  
工業電腦的應用場域多元化：隨著技術的演進，工業電腦的應用場景早已跨越工廠的圍牆，在各個專業領域的解決方案
成功落地，從車載應用、軌道交通、船舶海事、無人載具、人型機器人、工業機器人、工業⾃動化、5G通訊網路到邊境
安防，工業電腦的應用場域多元化，隨處可見。 
工業電腦的分類與應用場景

資料來源 : Google，玉山投顧整理、編製 
⾃從工業電腦從工廠走進我們生活日常的POS、交通售票系統、醫療診斷到博弈娛樂等，它的重要性就日益加重，舉例
來說 ， 於智慧醫療其應用於影像診斷與手術輔助 ， 核⼼在於精準度與醫療級電氣安全 ， 錯誤的成本極高 ； 智慧交通方面 ，
車載與軌道監控需⻑時間運算與抗環境⼲擾，任何中斷都直接影響公共安全。 
全球工業電腦市場2035年將達104億美元，工業電腦台廠各有專精：根據研調機構構Research Nester統計與預估，全
球工業電腦市場規模將由2025年的61億美元，成⻑至2035年的104億美元，年均複合成⻑率為7.9%，台廠從上游半導
體至下游電⼦組裝產業供應鏈完整，具上下游垂直整合優勢且各有專精。 
全球工業電腦市場2035年將達104億美元

資料來源 : Research Nester，玉山投顧整理、繪製

### Page 8

8 
台廠具上下游垂直整合優勢

資料來源 : Google，玉山投顧整理  
AI時代帶來邊緣運算需求 
AI時代到來，工業電腦將從硬體代工走向邊緣運算平台化，軟體的整合能力將決定產品毛利率的高低，工業電腦也從第
⼀代的控制功能跨步到第⼆代的連網功能，再進而邁向大步往第三代的人工智慧及時推理功能前進。未來，工業電腦已
不再只是單純的控制設備，而是逐步成為集資料蒐集、即時運算與通訊於⼀⾝的邊緣中樞；定位於邊緣運算讓工業電腦
不再只是資料收集設備，而是真正具備「看得見、聽得懂、想得出判斷」的能力，能在工廠端即時完成視覺檢測與設備
健康判讀；更關鍵的是，這種結合正在實際解決⻑期困擾製造業的問題，那就是「經驗無法複製」，透過 AI 學習資深
工匠的判斷邏輯，工業電腦得以把隱性知識轉為可執行的數位規則，即使在技術人力短缺的情況下，生產品質依然能被
穩定維持。 
工業電腦的世代演進

資料來源 : Google，玉山投顧整理、編製 
⾃從NVIDIA執行⻑黃仁勳宣告 「每⼀家工業公司都將成為機器人公司」，並將實體AI定義為繼生成式AI之後的下⼀波科
技浪潮後，AI便不再只是雲端的虛擬智慧，而是能感知環境、理解物理規律、並在真實世界採取行動的能力，這句話所
指向的核⼼硬體，正是工業電腦。未來工業電腦由於內建NPU、GPU等AI加速模組，將可在本地端即時分析影像、震動
等高頻資料，避免等待回傳結果，具有即時推論的效能；另外，也具備低延遲響應且降低頻寬需求，再加上模型的持續
優化，讓工業電腦扮演邊緣AI推論平台，除了整合機器視覺系統的算力核⼼，同時也協作機器人與人形機器人的控制大
腦。邊緣運算正在讓工業電腦從「事後記錄者」轉變為「即時預警者」，工業電腦不再只是設備，而會成為對抗全球勞
動力短缺、突破職人經驗瓶頸的關鍵工具，推動智慧製造從「改善」走向真正的「質變」，預估全球邊緣運算的市場規
模於2033年將可突破千億美元，年均複合成⻑率將可達21%。

### Page 9

9 
全球邊緣運算的市場規模於2033年將可突破千億美元

資料來源 : Precedence Research，玉山投顧整理、繪製 
工業電腦(IPC)3.0於2026年後將進入主體AI時代

資料來源 : 樺漢法說會資料 
工業電腦歷經成⻑及轉型升級 ， IPC 1.0 (2011至2015年)是工業電腦時代 ， 主要定義讓設備具備運算能力 。 IPC 2.0 (2016
至2020年)是工業物聯網時代，主要是讓設備彼此連結。而IPC 3.0 (2020至2025年)為智慧平台時代，目地是讓數據產生
價值。2026年之後進入主體AI時代，將讓AI從數據分析走向實際執行，從協助決策走向⾃主行動。 
各種實體AI與邊緣運算場景

資料來源 : 樺漢法說會資料

whoscall海外市場持續擴張
⚫ whoscall海外市場擴張成效卓越，海外營收占比於2025年已突破63%。泰國為
Gogolook最大的海外市場，其用戶規模、營收貢獻已超越台灣，但仍具備很大的
成長空間；公司於曼谷擁有龐大用戶基礎，目前正進軍泰國二、三線城市。菲律
賓與馬來西亞等地已具備百萬等級的活躍用戶，後續成長可期。
⚫ Gogolook於09/2026已正式和泰國政府退休基金(Government Pension Fund，GPF)簽
署MOU，並與國家跨行交易管理與交換公司(National ITMX)及泰國存款保障機構
(Deposit Protection Agency，DPA)合作，將whoscall防詐服務導入GPF體系，涵蓋
退休人員及現職公務員(超過120萬名會員)。此合作有助於同步推廣泰國當地B2C、
B2B業務。
⚫ 為了有效控制獲客成本，Gogolook在海外減少依賴成效較低的數位廣告，轉而與
當地政府、銀行及電信商合作。此外，考量到東南亞信用卡普及率較低，

Gogolook在泰國率先推出的「電信帳單扣款」訂閱模式大獲成功，目前此模式也
與台灣遠傳電信合作，藉此攻佔中高年齡層客群。

曼谷已站穩根基，
目前進軍二、三線
城市，並擴大與泰
國政府合作。主要
成長動能來源。
已具備百萬用戶
基礎，為後續重
點發展國家。

### Page 10

10
Gogolook收購ScamAdviser
⚫ 企業端防詐方面，由於各國法規有加重企業防詐責任的趨勢，Gogolook將此業務
視為長期成長動能潛力來源。Gogolook於2024年收購具備超過6,000萬筆網域資料
庫的ScamAdviser後，成功拓展歐美市場。儘管2025年因主動調整部分低毛利率業
務，導致ScamAdviser營收下滑，但其實質獲利反而增加。
資料來源：Gogolook、群益投顧彙整
⚫ ScamAdviser成立於荷蘭阿姆斯
特丹，為全球防詐聯盟GASA
基石成員。ScamAdviser以AI演
算法與資料庫為基礎，開發自
有的網站風險評級技術，與
Gogolook的企業防詐解決方案
與電話號碼資料庫整合，為全
球最完整的通訊與數位防詐數
據資料庫。

### Page 11

11
Gogolook企業端防詐服務
⚫ 企業端服務主要分為「資料庫授權」與「商譽保護」兩大主軸：(1) 防詐情資方案，
為一種資料庫授權模式，將whoscall的「電話/簡訊」資料庫以及ScamAdviser的
「網域」資料庫，透過API、SDK方式授權給企業。(2) 商譽保護服務，企業的外
部防詐監控中心，協助企業或政府主動在網路上巡邏，偵測並下架冒用其品牌的
假投資廣告、假粉專或釣魚網站。
資料來源：Gogolook、群益投顧彙整

### Page 12

12
Agent looker
⚫ 因應Agentic AI時代來臨，Gogolook於09/2026推出新產品Agent looker，工作原理
是在AI agent實際造訪或讀取網頁前，即時進行風險檢測並攔截惡意請求，可有效
避免AI agent被詐騙的風險。
⚫ 核心防護機制與功能：(1)毫秒級即時掃描，當AI Agent讀取外部網頁或執行URL
請求時，Agent Looker會在毫秒內對網頁封包與內容進行深度檢查，攔截惡意腳本、
釣魚網站以及針對 AI 模型的提示詞注入(Prompt Injection)攻擊。(2)Gogolook威脅
情資資料庫驅動。系統底層串接 Gogolook 累積多年的全球防詐騙、釣魚網站與風
險網域黑名單。基於海量真實風險樣本的數據庫，使其能快速且精準地辨識異常
行為。(3)活動稽核日誌。提供視覺化的管理儀表板，完整記錄AI Agent的所有外
部網路互動軌跡，包含造訪過的網址、讀取的網頁片段及最終的安全判定結果，
確保AI運作透明且可追溯。
⚫ Agent looker目前處於封閉測試(Private Beta)階段。

資料來源：Gogolook、群益投顧彙整

### Page 13

13
金融科技-袋鼠金融
⚫ 金融科技為Gogolook於疫情期間因應線上詐騙激增而開創的新業務，包含全台灣
流量最大的金融媒合平台「袋鼠金融Roo.Cash」，以及專攻小額借款的「JUJI招財
麻吉」。袋鼠金融 Roo.Cash與國內金融機構合作，提供200件以上的金融商品方案
智慧比較與媒合。智能問答服務「Roo AI」，可協助用戶在信用卡、貸款、保險、
投資理財與民生優惠資訊中，快速找到最適合的選項。若順利成交，Gogolook即
可藉此獲得媒合收入。
資料來源：Gogolook、群益投顧彙整

### Page 14

14
金融科技-JUJI招財麻吉
⚫ JUJI招財麻吉鎖定10萬元以下、傳統銀行不願承作的利基市場，自行舉辦機車貸
款、手機貸款等業務。該業務最核心的優勢在於將whoscall的通話行為數據轉化為
AI風控模型，不僅可嚴格控制核貸率，更將90天延滯率壓在極低水準。目前JUJI
招財麻吉應收分期款餘額持續增加，公司同時也把純風控模型顧問服務輸出至海
外市場，進一步擴大變現能力，2Q26已成功在泰國市場簽訂首批企業訂單。
資料來源：Gogolook、群益投顧彙整

### Page 16

16
Gogolook營收續增，毛利率維持高檔
⚫ 隨著消費者防詐、企業端防詐、金融科技三大事業皆穩定增長，預估營收呈現穩
定增長趨勢。觀察重點：whoscall於各國推展進度、企業對於agent looker接受度、
招財麻吉貸放額度及壞帳率變化、AI風控模型專案。

### Page 17

17
Gogolook營業費用控管得宜
⚫ 低成本合作取量：減少高成本數位廣告，擴大與政府單位、電信商、銀行合作，
低成本觸及海外客戶。
⚫ AI優化營運效率：利用AI提升工程與內部效率，人力控管但產出持續提升。
⚫ 費用率逐步下滑：隨著營收規模提升，費用控管得宜，2024年營業費用率96.78%，
2025年營業費用率82.29%，預估2026年營業費用率69.21%。
資料來源：群益投顧彙整

本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我們並不確保此資訊
的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依
賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究
報告的目的，既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價
服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可能會有無預警地
上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的情形。

隨客製化需求持續成長、柬埔寨廠人力恢復至5,000人並開始協助
客戶回補庫存，帶動3Q26營收創下歷史新高，且受惠於營收規模
擴大、產能利用率提升及關稅返還挹注，淨利率有望創高。展望後
市，公司持續擴增墨西哥產能，員工人數已達1,800人，後續預計
每年增加約 500 人，以改善交期並滿足客製化窗簾訂單成長需求；
越南新廠預計 2027年初投產，第一期規劃員工約 1,000人，可望
進一步擴大客製化產品產能與市佔率。市場需求方面，儘管美國市
場面臨高通膨及消費降級壓力，但公司憑藉穩健的財務結構，客製
化產品與同業價差已擴大至 30%～40%，具價格競爭優勢，有助
於提升市佔率及整體訂單量，預期維持 mid-single digit成長；固
定尺寸則透過新品推出，支撐營收維持 low-single digit成長；歐
洲市場方面，隨荷蘭、比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，可望延續
high-single digit 成長。獲利方面，受惠於關稅稅率降至 20%、
漲價及營收規模效益逐步顯現，預期毛利率將延續成長趨勢。綜合
以上，研究部預估 2026、2027 年 EPS 為 23.92、27.91 元，以
16倍本益比評價，給予目標價 450元，投資建議為買進。  
 近期投資評等  2Q26 毛利率創高，惟油價及運價上漲壓抑獲利表現 
2Q26 營收 79.27 億元，YoY+2.86%，QoQ+8.52%，稅後損
益 13.91 億元，YoY+20.67%，QoQ-15.57%，EPS 4.75 元，
低於研究部預期。第二季為傳統消費旺季，營運表現較第一季
明顯回升。區域營收方面，北美市場受到消費降級影響，加上
柬埔寨廠人力短缺，營收YoY-2.1%；非美市場則受惠於荷蘭、
比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，營收 YoY+19.8%，成為主
要成長動能。獲利方面，2Q26 毛利率 59.95%，YoY+0.7ppt，
QoQ+0.9ppt，受惠於客制化及歐洲市場營收比重持續提升，
帶動毛利率創歷史新高，惟油價及燃油附加費急漲，使費用率
升至 32.7%，抵銷部分毛利率改善效益，壓抑整體獲利表現。

 柬埔寨廠產能恢復，固定尺寸產品開始回補庫存

公司客製化窗簾產能主要布局於中國及墨西哥，分別占整體產

哥則鎖定北美市場，截至 2026 年 9 月，舊廠與新廠合計員工

題並因應市佔擴張需求，透過規模經濟及生產效率提升，進一
步優化整體獲利結構。柬埔寨廠則以固定尺寸窗簾為主，1H26
受缺工影響，出貨進度放緩， 6M26 起員工人數已由先前的
4,000 人回升至 5,000人，並開始協助客戶回補庫存至健康水
位。越南新廠預計於 2027 年初投產，聚焦客制化產品，第一
期規劃員工人數約 1,000 人，為中長期成長動能。

 同業價差擴大提升競爭力，歐洲市場成長動能延續 
儘管美國消費市場面臨高通膨、消費降級及關稅等壓力，但公
司憑藉穩健的財務結構，與同業的價差已擴大至30%～40%，
加上產品持續迭代更新，有助於提升市佔率及整體訂單量，預
估 2026~2027 年客製化業務可維持 mid-single digit成長；固
定尺寸則透過新品免打孔窗廉推出，提高平均售價，以維持
low-single digit 成長。歐洲市場方面，採線上直營電商模式，
專業人員到府量測與安裝服務，由於服務利潤率較高，已成為
主要獲利來源。目前已拓展至荷蘭、比利時、丹麥等市場，隨
覆蓋率快速提升，預期非美地區將延續 high-single digit 成
長。電動窗簾方面，價格為手動款的 2～2.5 倍，儘管目前佔
比仍為 low-single digit，但 CAGR 達 15%～20%以上，預期
未來營收占比有望提升至 10%，進一步帶動整體 ASP 及產品
組合優化。

料使用比重，降低對 PVC 價格波動的依賴，以減輕短期原料

劃，有助於緩解成本上升壓力，加上客製化產品營收比重逐步
擴大、關稅稅率降至 20%，預期全年毛利率仍將維持成長趨
勢。綜合以上，研究部預估  2026 年營收 322.33 億元，
YoY+7.56%，稅後損益 70.1 億元，YoY+6.19%，EPS 23.92
元；2027 年營收 351.08億元，YoY+8.92%，稅後損益 81.77
億元，YoY+16.65%，EPS 27.91 元。

 結論與建議 
隨客製化需求持續成長、柬埔寨廠人力恢復至5,000人並開始
協助客戶回補庫存，帶動3Q26營收創下歷史新高，且受惠於
營收規模擴大、產能利用率提升及關稅返還挹注，淨利率有望
創高。展望後市，公司持續擴增墨西哥產能，員工人數已達
1,800人，後續預計每年增加約500人，以改善交期並滿足客製
化窗簾訂單成長需求；越南新廠預計2027年初投產，第一期規
劃員工約1,000人，可望進一步擴大客製化產品產能與市佔
率。市場需求方面，儘管美國市場面臨高通膨及消費降級壓
力，但公司憑藉穩健的財務結構，客製化產品與同業價差已擴
大至30%～40%，具價格競爭優勢，有助於提升市佔率及整體
訂單量，預期維持mid-single digit成長；固定尺寸則透過新品
推出，支撐營收維持low-single digit成長；歐洲市場方面，隨
荷蘭、比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，可望延續high-single 
digit成長。獲利方面，受惠於關稅稅率降至20%、漲價及營收
規模效益逐步顯現，預期毛利率將延續成長趨勢。綜合以上，
研究部預估2026、2027年EPS為23.92、27.91元，以16倍本
益比評價，給予目標價450元，投資建議為買進。

## Document 181: 9月经济数据点评：生产偏强、需求渐进修复

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月经济数据点评：生产偏强、需求渐进修复.pdf
Report source: 联储证券
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告 
宏观经济点评 2026 年 10 月 17 日

请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明

9 月经济数据点评：生产偏强、需求渐进修复

[Table_Author] 
 魏争 分析师  沈夏宜 分析师

Email:weizheng@lczq.com Email:shenxiayi@lczq.com  
证书:S1320524100001 证书:S1320523020004

### Page 2

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 2 
6.2%升至 9.8%，我们理解部分原因是地缘局势提高能源安全和保供要
求，国内油气增储上产持续推进，为采矿业生产提供了支撑 。二是电热燃
水业增速回升。电力、热力、燃气及水生产和供应业增加值增长 5.6%，
较 8 月提高 0.7 个百分点。从实物产量看，核电、风电和太阳能发电分别
增长 21.5%、10.6%和 19.5%。 
服务业生产明显改善，生产性服务业延续支撑，生活性服务有修复。9
月，服务业生产指数同比增长 4.8%，较 8 月提高 0.7 个百分点；一是数
字信息和生产配套型服务业继续保持较快增长。 信息传输软件和信息技术
服务业、租赁和商务服务业、交通运输仓储和邮政业生产指数 分别增长
9.8%、9.5%和 5.4%。二是服务业改善广度有提升。住宿和餐饮业生产指
数增长 5.3%，服务业商务活动指数由 49.3%升至 50.2%，重新进入扩张
区间；电信广播电视及卫星传输、货币金融和保险等行业商务活动指数均
位于 55%以上1。 
投资低位企稳。9 月，固定资产投资累计同比下降 7.2%，降幅与 8 月持
平。按累计金额反推，9 月当月投资同比约下降 7.2%，较 8 月的下降
11.0%明显收窄；剔除房地产后，当月投资同比约下降 3.4%，降幅较 8
月收窄约 4.4 个百分点。季调环比增长 0.07%，是 3 月以来首次转正。 
制造业投资小幅修复，增长仍集中在少数优势领域 。9 月，制造业累计投
资下降 2.1%，降幅较 1—8 月收窄 0.2 个百分点。结构上看，一是正增行
业仍主要集中在装备和新兴制造领域。国家统计局公布的 13 个制造业行
业中，只有 5 个保持正增长，其中铁路船舶航空航天、计算机通信电子设
备制造业投资分别增长 15.8%和 9.6%，纺织、电气机械和通用设备制造
业分别增长 4.2%、1.2%和 1.1%。高技术产业投资增长 4.9%，其中信息
服务、航空航天器及设备、电子及通信设备制造业投资分别增长 20.2%、
12.5%和 7.6%。二是上游原材料和下游消费制造业投资仍较弱。农副食
品加工、食品制造、化工、医药、有色金属冶炼、金属制品、专用设备和
汽车制造业投资分别下降 2.5%、10.3%、7.5%、8.1%、5.1%、10.1%、
8.9%和 6.3%。 
地产继续承压，价格端修复难抵销售走弱。9 月，地产投资累计同比下降
20.6%，降幅较 8 月扩大 0.7 个百分点；按累计金额反推，9 月当月投资
下降约 26.3%，降幅较 8 月扩大约 0.8 个百分点。结构上看，一是地产销
售再次走弱，约束投资预期。 商品房销售面积和销售额累计分别下降
13.2%和 13.9%，降幅较 1—8 月扩大 1.1 和 0.9 个百分点；按累计金额反
推，9 月当月销售面积和销售额分别下降约 20.7%和 20.1%，较 8 月的下
降 14.8%和 12.1%明显走弱。东部地区销售额降幅由 12.6%扩大至
13.4%。30 大中城市商品房成交面积同比下降 10.9%，较 8 月扩大约 7.5
个百分点，其中一线城市增速由 18.8%降至 2.4%，二线城市降幅由
15.8%扩大至 22.7%，三线城市降幅基本持平。二是供给和资金端仍然偏
弱。新开工面积累计下降 24.9%，降幅较 8 月扩大 0.1 个百分点，竣工面
积降幅由 23.7%扩大至 25.1%。房企到位资金下降 21.6%，降幅扩大 0.6

### Page 3

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 3 
个百分点，其中，自筹资金降幅由 20.0%扩大至 21.4%；国内贷款和个人
按揭贷款降幅则分别小幅收窄至 33.1%和 22.1%。三是价格端有积极变
化。9 月，70 城新房和二手房价格同比分别下降 3.0%和 4.8%，降幅均较
8 月收窄 0.3 个百分点；环比看，新房降幅由 0.2%收窄至 0.1%，二手房
下降 0.3%，与上月持平。一线城市新房和二手房价格分别上涨 0.1%和
0.2%，二线城市新房价格由下降转为持平、二手房降幅收窄。 
基建投资继续放缓，项目端景气 有改善迹象。9 月，基建投资累计同比下
降 4.2%，降幅较 8 月扩大 0.2 个百分点。一方面，部分传统基建领域仍
然偏弱。交通运输仓储和邮政业投资增长 1.9%，较前期回落 0.2 个百分
点；水利环境和公共设施管理业投资下降 12.8%，降幅扩大 1.8 个百分
点，其中水利管理和公共设施管理业均下降 12%以上。电热燃水投资下降
6.8%，降幅较前期小幅收窄。另一方面，项目储备和部分结构性领域出现
积极信号，但向施工和实物工作量的传导仍不充分。9 月，建筑业商务活
动指数由 46.9%升至 50.3%，从业人员和业务活动预期指数也有所提高。
信息传输、航空运输和水上运输投资分别增长 30.8%、13.8%和 13.5%，
交通、能源、数据等“六张网”相关领域仍有支撑。 建筑业新订单指数由
42.4%升至 45.7%，尚未回到扩张区间，指向前端项目需求有改善，但向
施工和实物工作量的传导仍不充分。 
消费是内需端改善最明显的分项，商品消费仍受汽车拖累 。9 月，社零同
比增长 1.1%，较 8 月提高 0.7 个百分点，环比增长 0.35%，剔除汽车后
社零同比增长 3.9%，较 8 月加快 1.4 个百分点。结构上看，商品和餐饮
消费同步恢复。9 月，商品零售额增长 1.0%，较 8 月提高 0.7 个百分点；
餐饮收入增长 2.1%，提高 1.0 个百分点。从更广义的消费口径看，服务
消费仍是消费的重要支撑。三季度，社会消费商品和服务零售总额增长
2.6%，其中服务零售额增长 5.0%，明显高于商品零售额的 1.0%；网上
服务零售额增长 5.3%。通讯信息、旅游咨询租赁和文体休闲服务零售额
保持较快增长。商品消费方面，必选消费和通讯器材维持韧性，汽车是主
要拖累。服装、化妆品、日用品、家电和中西药品零售额分别增长
1.2%、5.4%、4.1%、1.9%和 5.0%，通讯器材类增长 31.1%。汽车零售
额下降 21.2%，降幅较 8 月扩大 2.7 个百分点；家具、建筑装潢材料仍分
别下降 3.0%和 9.2%，降幅较 8 月有所收窄。 
展望后续，四季度经济有望延续生产较强、需求渐进修复的格局，未来增
量支撑主要来自两条线索。总体看，三季度实际增长边际企稳，优势制造
业、外需和服务消费构成主要支撑。往后看，增量支撑线索有两方面。一
方面，以“六张网”和 8000 亿元新型政策性金融工具为抓手，加快资金
投放和项目开工。9 月以来三家政策性银行投放的新型政策性金融工具资
金已经超过 1000 亿元2，建筑业 PMI 和新订单指数同步回升，指向政策
正在向项目端传导。不过，从补充资本金、落实配套融资到形成施工和实
物工作量仍存在时滞，基建对传统建筑链的拉动或更多在四季度后半段逐
步显现。另一方面，服务消费仍是扩大内需更具确定性的方向。9 月多部
门出台金融支持服务业扩能提质政策，叠加扩大消费“十五五”规划对养

### Page 4

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 4 
老、托育、文旅、健康等领域的部署，服务消费有望继续快于商品消费。

风险提示：外需边际走弱、地缘政治超预期恶化、AI 产业链降温超预期、
政策资金投放节奏不及预期。

### Page 7

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 7 
1. GDP：实际增长边际回升 
实际 GDP 边际回升， 名义增长继续快于实际增长。三季度经济增长呈现三个特征，
一是实际增长较二季度有改善。 三季度实际GDP 同比增长 4.5%， 高于二季度的4.3%，
但仍略低于上半年平均水平；前三季度 GDP 累计增长 4.6%，较上半年回落 0.1 个百分
点。分产业看，实际 GDP 回升主要来自第二产业改善。第二产业增加值增速由二季度
的 3.0%回升至 3.4%， 第三产业由5.1%小幅升至 5.2%， 第一产业则由3.7%回落至 3.6%。
二是名义 GDP 继续快于实际 GDP，第二产业价格修复仍是重要支撑。 根据现价计算，
三季度名义 GDP 同比增长约 6.0%，较二季度提高 0.1 个百分点，高于实际 GDP 增速
1.5 个百分点。按名义与实际增速差近似估算，三季度 GDP 平减指数约为 1.5%，较二
季度的 1.6%略有回落， 指向整体价格修复仍在延续， 但没有进一步加快。 结构上， 第二
产业价格为主要支撑。二季度，第一产业平减指数由-3.3%回升至-1.7%，第三产业则由
1.3%回落至 0.7%， 第二产业平减指数约3.0%升至 3.6%， 其中工业由3.8%上升至 4.4%，
其表现与国际能源价格上涨以及 AI 产业链、 有色和电子设备价格较强基本一致。三是内
需端对经济增长的支撑边际回落，其中投资为最大拖累，外需端支撑边际增强。 支出法
看， 前三季度最终消费支出对经济增长的贡献率为45.7%， 较上半年回落0.3 个百分点；
资本形成总额贡献率为 28.3%，较上半年回落 7.8 个百分点；货物和服务净出口贡献率
为 26.1%，较上半年提升 8.2 个百分点。

请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 8 
2. 生产：工业继续回升 
工业生产继续回升，采矿业和电热燃水业改善显著。9 月，规上工业增加值同比增
长 5.5%，较 8 月提高 0.3 个百分点，环比增长 0.6%。总量上看，出口对工业生产的拉
动延续。9 月，出口交货值同比增长 17.0%，较 8 月提高 5.9 个百分点。结构上看，工
业生产回升有两方面原因。一方面，制造业增加值维持韧性，高增长仍集中在装备制造
和高技术制造领域。9 月，制造业增加值增长 6.0%，较 8 月小幅回落 0.1 个百分点，但
仍高于整体工业增速。其中，高技术制造业增长 15.3%；金属制品、通用设备、专用设
备、 铁路船舶航空航天、 电气机械和计算机通信电子设备制造业分别增长 8.2%、8.5%、
12.1%、11.7%、8.1%和 16.4%。下游消费制造业总体保持正增。农副食品加工、食品
制造、 酒饮料和精制茶、 纺织、 医药和汽车制造业分别增长4.1%、7.3%、5.5%、3.2%、
4.9%和 6.7%。另一方面，拖累项增加值边际修复。一是采矿业由负转正，对工业生产
的拖累明显减轻。9 月，采矿业增加值由下降 1.4%转为增长 1.3%，其中煤炭开采和洗
选业降幅由 5.6%收窄至 3.3%，石油和天然气开采业增速由 6.2%升至 9.8%，我们理解
部分原因是地缘局势提高能源安全和保供要求，国内油气 增储上产持续推进，为采矿业
生产提供了支撑。二是电热燃水业增速回升。电力、热力、燃气及水生产和供应业增加
值增长 5.6%，较 8 月提高 0.7 个百分点。 从实物产量看， 核电、 风电和太阳能发电分别
增长 21.5%、10.6%和 19.5%。 
服务业生产明显改善，生产性服务业延续支撑，生活性服务有修复。 9 月，服务业
生产指数同比增长 4.8%，较8 月提高 0.7 个百分点； 一是数字信息和生产配套型服务业
继续保持较快增长。信息传输软件和信息技术服务业、租赁和商务服务业、交通运输仓
储和邮政业生产指数分别增长 9.8%、9.5%和 5.4%。二是服务业改善广度有提升。 住宿
和餐饮业生产指数增长 5.3%，服务业商务活动指数由 49.3%升至 50.2%，重新进入扩
张区间； 电信广播电视及卫星传输、 货币金融和保险等行业商务活动指数均位于55%以
上。

3. 投资：延续回落 
投资低位企稳。9 月， 固定资产投资累计同比下降7.2%， 降幅与 8 月持平。按累计
金额反推，9 月当月投资同比约下降 7.2%，较 8 月的下降 11.0%明显收窄； 剔除房地产
后， 当月投资同比约下降3.4%， 降幅较8 月收窄约 4.4 个百分点。 季调环比增长0.07%，
是 3 月以来首次转正。 
图8 固定资产投资低位企稳（%）

### Page 10

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 10 
制造业投资小幅修复， 增长仍集中在少数优势领域。9 月， 制造业累计投资下降2.1%，
降幅较 1—8 月收窄 0.2 个百分点。 结构上看， 一是正增行业仍主要集中在装备和新兴制
造领域。国家统计局公布的 13 个制造业行业中，只有 5 个保持正增长，其中铁路船舶
航空航天、 计算机通信电子设备制造业投资分别增长15.8%和 9.6%， 纺织、 电气机械和
通用设备制造业分别增长 4.2%、1.2%和 1.1%。高技术产业投资增长 4.9%，其中信息
服务、 航空航天器及设备、 电子及通信设备制造业投资分别增长20.2%、12.5%和 7.6%。
二是上游原材料和下游消费制造业投资仍较弱。 农副食品加工、 食品制造、 化工、 医药、
有色金属冶炼、 金属制品、 专用设备和汽车制造业投资分别下降2.5%、10.3%、7.5%、
8.1%、5.1%、10.1%、8.9%和 6.3%。

4. 消费：改善相对明显，服务强于商品 
消费是内需端改善最明显的分项， 商品消费仍受汽车拖累。9 月， 社零同比增长1.1%，
较 8 月提高 0.7 个百分点，环比增长 0.35%，剔除汽车后社零同比增长 3.9%，较 8 月
加快 1.4 个百分点。 结构上看， 商品和餐饮消费同步恢复。9 月， 商品零售额增长1.0%，
较 8 月提高 0.7 个百分点； 餐饮收入增长2.1%， 提高1.0 个百分点。 从更广义的消费口
径看， 服务消费仍是消费的重要支撑。 三季度， 社会消费商品和服务零售总额增长2.6%，
其中服务零售额增长 5.0%， 明显高于商品零售额的 1.0%； 网上服务零售额增长5.3%。
通讯信息、旅游咨询租赁和文体休闲服务零售额保持较快增长。商品消费方面，必选消
费和通讯器材维持韧性，汽车是主要拖累。服装、化妆品、日用品、家电和中西药品零
售额分别增长 1.2%、5.4%、4.1%、1.9%和 5.0%，通讯器材类增长 31.1%。汽车零售
额下降 21.2%， 降幅较8 月扩大 2.7 个百分点； 家具、 建筑装潢材料仍分别下降3.0%和
9.2%，降幅较 8 月有所收窄。 
图15 9 月社零增速边际修复（%）

5. 展望：有望延续生产较强、内需修复格局 
展望后续，四季度经济有望延续生产较强、需求渐进修复的格局，未来增量支撑主
要来自两条线索。总体看，三季度实际增长边际企稳，优势制造业、外需和服务消费构
成主要支撑。 往后看， 增量支撑线索有两方面。 一方面， 以 “六张网” 和8000 亿元新型
政策性金融工具为抓手，加快资金投放和项目开工。9 月以来三家政策性银行投放的新
型政策性金融工具资金已经超过 1000 亿元，建筑业 PMI 和新订单指数同步回升，指向
政策正在向项目端传导。不过，从补充资本金、落实配套融资到形成施工和实物工作量
仍存在时滞，基建对传统建筑链的拉动或更多在四季度后半段逐步显现。另一方面，服
务消费仍是扩大内需更具确定性的方向。9 月多部门出台金融支持服务业扩能提质政策，
叠加扩大消费“十五五”规划对养老、托育、文旅、健康等领域的部署，服务消费有望
继续快于商品消费。 
风险提示 
外需边际走弱、地缘政治超预期恶化、AI 产业链降温超预期、政策资金投放节奏不
及预期。

投资评级说明 
投资建议的评级标准  评级 说明 
评级标准为报告发布日后的 6 个月内公司股价 （或行业
指数） 相对同期基准指数的相对市场表现。 其中A 股市
场以沪深 300 指数为基准； 新三板市场以三板成指 （针
对协议转让标的） 或三板做市指数 （针对做市转让标的）
为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准。 
股票评级 
买入 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 10%以上 
增持 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 5%~10%之间 
中性 相对同期基准指数涨幅在-5%~+5%之间 
减持 相对同期基准指数跌幅在 5%以上 
行业评级 
看好 相对表现优于市场 
中性 相对表现与市场持平 
看淡 相对表现弱于市场

## Document 207: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

00

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 
기다렸던 4 행정 중속 엔진 캐파 증설 단행 
HD현대중공업의 4행정 엔진 캐파는 기존 3.0GW에서 증설이 완료되는 '28년부터 
램프업을 거쳐 '30년 7.2GW까지 확대될 계획이다. ①[육상 발전용]은 신공장(울산 
온산) 3GW 와 HD 현대엔진(전남 영암) 1GW 를 합쳐 4.0GW, ②[선박용]은 
2.3GW에서 3.2GW로 확대된다. HD현대엔진 캐파는 기존 0.7GW에서 1GW까
지 확대되며, 해당 라인은 신공장이 가동되기 전 ‘27~’28 년 구간에서의 선박&육상 엔
진 모두 대응 가능하다. 신공장 캐파는 총 3.0GW로, ‘28년 약 30%의 가동률로 시
작해 램프업을 거쳐 ‘30 년부터 풀캐파로 20MW급 모델을 생산할 예정이다. 4 행정 
중속엔진에 대한 수요 가시성은 높다. 동사가 공유했던 육상 발전 엔진 인콰이어리 규
모는 기존 약 3~9GW였으나, 현재는 이를 크게 상회하는 수준으로 소통하고 있다.

최근 부유식 데이터센터(FDC)에 대한 하이퍼스케일러들의 인콰이어리 또한 확대되고 
있다. HD 한국조선해양은 최근 미국 선급(ABS)로부터 100MW급 규모의 부유식 발
전 설비(BMPP)에 대한 기본 인증(AiP)를 획득했으며, 동사는 FDC 신조 사업과 더불
어 발전원 4 행정 엔진을 파워십 및 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)에 납품하며 그룹사들과 
패키지 사업으로 진행할 예정이다. 가시화된 수요를 기반으로 엔진기계사업부의 구조적 
성장 보여줄 것으로 전망하며, 이에 따른 리레이팅을 예상한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원 유지 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원을 유지한다. 동사는 
올해 상선수주 목표 이미 초과달성하며 안정적인 펀더멘털 유지 중이다. 하반기 남은 
기간은 LNGC, VLGC/VLAC 중심의 선별 수주 전략을 통해 ‘28 년 이후의 상선 부
문 수익성 유지 충분히 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 글로벌 지정학적 리스크 확대에 따른 
해군 현대화 사이클 기조에 맞추어 미 해군은 동맹국의 호위함 구매를 고려하고 있으
며, MASGA를 기반으로 중장기적으로 미국 전투함을 건조하기 위해 HD현대의 미국 
진출 가능성 높다는 판단이다. 또한 미국을 제외한 글로벌 함정(페루, 필리핀, 말레이시
아, 칠레, 사우디, 이집트 등) 수주 파이프라인도 다수 보유하고 있어 함정 모멘텀에 따
른 리레이팅 충분히 가능할 전망이다. 특수선+4 행정 엔진+FDC+SMR 등 조선소에 
붙일 수 있는 모든 추가 성장동력을 보유한 유일한 종목이기에 조선업 최선호주(Top-
Pick)로 제시한다. 최근 불거지고 있는 노조 파업 문제 해소될 시 저점 매수 기회라는 
판단이다.

HiMSEN 엔진 및 SMR 주기기 생산설비 대규모 증설 발표 
- 9/10 HD현대중공업은 총 1조 722억원 규모의 시설투자 2건을 공시 
① 대출력 발전엔진(4행정 중속엔진, HiMSEN엔진) 생산능력 확충에 8,336
억원 투입, 글로벌 전력수요 증가에 따른 엔진발전 시장 확대에 대응하는 목
적. 대출력 발전엔진 전용 공장 신설과 토지 취득, 건축물·기계설비 등 생산설
비 구축이 골자. 투자기간은 ‘26.09~’28.05(설계/인허가 ~’27.02, 공장건설 
~’28.05. ’28.06 상업운전개시, 28년 가동률 30%, 2030년 100% 도달) 
② SMR 주기기 제작 생산시설 구축에 2,386억원(자기자본 대비 2.55%) 투
입, SMR 주기기 제작사업 진입 및 수주 물량 증가에 대응. SMR 전용 공장 
및 설비 신설과 기존 공장 최적화를 병행, 투자기간은 ’26.10~’29.04. 
기다리던 증설 공시. 늘어난 Capa로 AI DC향 수혜 확대 기대 
- 25년 3.0GW(선박용 2.3GW + 육상용 0.7GW)였던 HiMSEN 엔진 생산 
Capa는 2030년 7.2GW(선박용 3.2GW + 육상용 4.0GW)로 확대. 그간 선
박용 중심이었던 엔진 사업의 포트폴리오를 발전용으로 본격 다각화하는 사
업 재편으로 해석 
- AI DC 신규 증설 확대 추세 속 계통 연결의 어려움과 CCGT, Aero -
derivative 대비 납기 및 부하상승시간, 모듈화 용이성 영역에서 장점을 가진 
발전용 중속엔진에 대한 수요 지속 기대(260908 당사 보고서 참조) 
- 지난 Corban Energy Group 향 계약 건(1,000MW, $ 674 mn)을 단순 
적용 시 금번 증설의 향후 매출액 기여규모는 연간 $ 2.8 bn에 달함. 엔진 시
장의 Seller-Market 기조에 따라 20%를 상회하는 높은 이익률이 지속될 것
으로 전망함에 따라 향후 유의미한 이익 성장 기대 
- HD현대마린솔루션 또한 LTSA 수주 저변 확대 측면에서 향후 이익 확장 가
능. HiMSEN엔진 고유 라이선스 보유에 따른 시너지로 그룹사 전반의 수혜 
기대

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
• Central banks’ communication is carefully scrutinised for
every nuance. Lately, there seems to have been more RBA
communication to pore over. The method of delivery has
also evolved. This has sometimes caused confusion when
tone seems to vary. Different audiences call for different

tone and varying complexity.
• The content has evolved, too. RBA officials are increasingly
using speeches directed to professional audiences to
discuss more technical estimates of inferred variables such
as neutral rates and financial conditions. They are also
highlighting that monetary policy combats inflation in more
ways than just squeezing mortgage holders.
• One thing has not changed: the RBA is not in the business
of providing ‘forward guidance’ promises about the future

path of interest rates. It does, however, give more running
commentary about how it is interpreting recent data. These
interpretations are themselves useful signals about what
the RBA thinks it might do.
Market participants pore over every utterance by central banks
for clues to future rate moves. They will have plenty to pore over
in the run-up to the November RBA decision. After the flurry
of fireside chats this week, ’60 Minutes’ will run a pre-recorded

interview with chief economist Sarah Hunter on Sunday. Next
Wednesday, the whole senior policy team will appear before
Senate Estimates, just as the quarterly CPI is released. (This
timing will have been the Senate’s choice, not the RBA’s.) After
the decision will come the Statement on Monetary Policy, a
media release and press conference. More speeches will follow,
including one by the Deputy Governor on the Thursday.
RBA communication does seem to have changed, not just

following the RBA Review but also more recently. There may
be fewer policy meetings each year, but the communication
outside that certainly feels more frequent than a couple of years
ago. Back in 2024, speaking engagements felt a little sparse
outside the post-meeting media conference and parliamentary
appearances. We have counted 58 public engagements so
far this year on the Speeches page on the RBA’s website.
This compares with 58 for the whole of last year and 52 in

2024. Excluding the ‘compulsory’ press conferences and
parliamentary appearances, fewer than half of the 2024 events
were related to economics, markets and monetary policy.
The ‘how’ has changed
The way the RBA communicates with markets has changed
as well. There are fewer set-piece speeches on policy and a
Open mouth operations
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
lot more semi-scripted ‘fireside chats’. Compared with a few
years ago, there are more interviews across a wide range of

media. This is a big change from the pre-Review world. It was
a huge deal when the previous Governor appeared on the
7:30 Report ; it is hard to imagine him agreeing to an Assistant
Governor appearing on 60 Minutes.
Part of the change reflects increased efforts to reach a general
audience. Central banks want to explain why they do what
they do. This matters especially when interest rates are rising
but the household sector is not the source of the inflation.

Speaking differently to different audiences can sometimes
confuse markets. As the RBA’s Chief Communication Officer
highlighted in a recent panel discussion, the post-meeting
media conference is designed to speak to the public. Market
participants and economists certainly tune in, but we are not
the primary audience. So, if you think that what the Governor
said in the media conference sounded more ‘dovish’ than
what another official says in a more market-oriented forum,

it is partly because she is trying to convey that the RBA
understands that its recent policy actions are making things
harder for many Australians. But this is in much the same way
that a doctor might say, “This might hurt a bit.” It does, but that
will not stop the treatment.
A related issue is that it is easier to control tone in pre-drafted
material such as the minutes and set-piece speeches than in
unstructured Q&A. Senior central bankers are trained to be

careful with their words in unscripted settings. If the tone
differs, the minutes are still more likely to say what the Board
really means than an off-the-cuff answer.
Greater communication with the broader public might have
reduced the pressure to make the set-piece speeches more
accessible. Research on central bank communication often
uses measures such as the Flesch–Kincaid Grade Level and
Flesch Reading Ease score. A comprehensive analysis was

beyond the scope of this note. However, a spot comparison
suggests four speeches from 2026 ( 1, 2, 3, 4) were harder
to read than four from 2021 (1, 2, 3, 4). The 2021 speeches
scored in the 11 to 12 range, which is high-school-level
“The RBA does not want to promise what it will
do, but it is increasingly trying to tell us what
it thinks. "

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
complexity. The 2026 speeches scored between 12 and
15, which is academic or technically dense text requiring
university-level education. (Including two of my own 2021
speeches is an obvious caveat, but the two speeches by then-
Governor Phil Lowe also scored better on these metrics than

the sample from this year. It should be noted, though, that
readability metrics do not necessarily imply better clarity if the
concepts discussed are more abstract.)
The ‘what’ has changed
As noted above, the content of RBA communication has
evolved, too. As its intellectual framework has begun to put
more weight on the balance of supply and demand, and those
invisible ‘star’ variables (NAIRUs, neutral real rates, ‘financial
conditions’, etc.), the communication to professional audiences

has increasingly focused on those topics.
More recently, the RBA has also placed more emphasis on
the full range of transmission channels from interest rates to
inflation (though not, as we have noted previously, the labour
supply channel). This is understandable in a world of supply
shocks. People need to understand that monetary policy is not
simply an exercise in hitting mortgage borrowers. We doubt
they would be comforted, though, by the Deputy Governor’s

explanation this week that the ‘cashflow channel’ was the
smallest of the transmission channels from interest rates
to inflation, with the exchange rate much more prominent.
That implies a lot of pain for a small group, for little effect on
inflation. It raises the question of whether other policy tools
or institutional arrangements might get the same effect on
inflation through the other channels without hitting a minority
of households disproportionately.

We have also seen a move away from using catchphrases
to summarise the position. From ‘narrow path’ to ‘not
ruling anything in or out’ to ‘cautious and predictable’, once
entrenched, a phrase can outlast its usefulness.
One thing that has not changed is the way the minutes record
the presentations to the Board and the members’ discussion. The
minutes rarely convey much new information beyond the post-
meeting statements. Sometimes, though, the minutes reveal the

Board’s reaction to the staff’s analysis. The August 2024 minutes
were a good example: the discussion highlighted just how
sensitive the forecasts were to key judgements by the staff.
The ‘why’ has not changed
Despite these changes, the underlying objectives of the RBA’s
communication have not changed. It wants to explain – and
perhaps justify – its decisions. And it wants to describe how
it interprets incoming data, especially inflation, the labour

market and the balance of supply and demand.
The RBA, like any central bank, is not really in the business
of providing ‘forward guidance’ about future moves.
Circumstances change, so it can never guarantee a particular
future path for interest rates. Some academic literature argues
that promising to keep rates low for a long time (or high,
for that matter) independently amplifies the transmission
of monetary policy beyond the effect of current-period

interest rates alone, by shifting the short end of the yield
curve. Central banks including the RBA have certainly tried
jawboning in this way from time to time. The prevailing view,
though, is that the potential cost of reputational loss if things
do not turn out as predicted outweighs the benefits of a
slightly stronger transmission mechanism.
In this context, it is the interpretation of data that provides
the signal about future policy directions. This is why it was

noteworthy that the Governor still characterised the labour
market as ‘a little tight’ after the September meeting, even
though the Board’s post-meeting communication did not. We
have been noting for some time that the RBA staff have a
downbeat view of supply capacity. Together with the RBA’s
oft-stated concerns about inflation, this is why the Deputy
Governor’s ‘hawkishness’ did not surprise us.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
While annual retail sales growth improved in September,
rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace of growth
is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very
weak versus history. Consumer spending continues to be

constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to
employment prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five
years into the property downturn, house prices continue to
decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep
downturn, –20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from
annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in high-tech
investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst
domestically oriented businesses as well as property’s

malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and
2028 as the contribution from net exports moderates, broad-
based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect recent
targeted support measures to be broadened and given much
greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr
in September, exceeding both market expectations and the
Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely
driven by energy, fuel prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core

inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services
inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an
immediate concern inflation, the labour market showing
resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to
4.7% in August, suggests demand risks for inflation will need
close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as
the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is released. For both events,

the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The
week after, the US mid-term elections are due.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’
attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor Hauser's fireside
chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a
follow-up rate hike at the November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook
was "materially worse" than the RBA's August forecasts.

Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having
"crystallised" ahead of the September meeting – the Middle
East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser
noted the Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices
are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in August”.
He then positioned AI and data centre developments among
an "extraordinary sequence of positive demand shocks around
the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through

into CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy
Governor noted that "inflation's been coming in at or a little
bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the
labour market". He went on to state that the staff are currently
assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement
on capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other
words, revise up their assumption for the NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of

market pricing for a follow-up November hike, from just a
20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition
today ahead of next week's Q3 CPI. For that release, we are
forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a
0.9 (3.5%yr) increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview
provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3 as
well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast.
Provided inflation prints broadly in line with our expectations,

the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.
Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3
and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to 3.7%yr. Under
the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely
watched measures of core inflation took a step higher to now
be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our
NZ economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise
the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October policy review, while

retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.
Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised  to the upside in
Q3, GDP growth accelerating from 0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting
annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on
track to achieve their 4.5–5.0% full-year growth objective.
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist
“With little domestic data of significance,
participants’ attention focused on RBA Deputy

Governor Hauser's fireside chat. Offshore,
China GDP was the key release. "

5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
RBNZ now expected to hike in October
We now expect the RBNZ will deliver a 25bp hike at its
policy review on 28 October. While September quarter
inflation was slightly below the RBNZ’s forecast, the
underlying detail was firm. More recently, increases in

fuel prices point to a material lift in inflation by year-end.
We expect further interest rate increases over the coming
months, with the OCR reaching a peak of 4% next year.
Softer headline inflation but firm core pressures
leave RBNZ on course to hike in October
We now expect the RBNZ to raise the Official Cash Rate
by 25bp at its October policy review (previously, we had
expected a pause at this meeting). This change in view reflects
significant upside risks to near-term inflation relative to the

RBNZ’s previous forecast.
While in line with our forecast, the September quarter inflation
figures were slightly softer than the RBNZ expected, with prices
rising 3.7% over the past year (compared with the RBNZ’s
forecast of 3.9%). However, the details were less reassuring.
Excluding volatile items such as food and fuel, underlying
inflation pressures remained firm. Closely watched measures
of core inflation moved higher and now sit close to, or a little

above, the top of the RBNZ’s target band. We also saw an
increase in the number of goods recording price rises, taking
that measure back to levels seen in the wake of the pandemic.
Alongside the existing firmness in underlying inflation, the
risks for inflation over the coming quarters are firmly tilted to
the upside. That near-term upside risk has also been noted by
the Governor. Following a sharp rise in global fuel costs, we
expect inflation to reach 4.3% by the end of this year (above

the RBNZ’s September MPS forecast for +3.9%). While that is
mainly due to higher prices at the pump, businesses in sectors
such as construction are also reporting related increases in
transport and materials costs. Consistent with those trends,
we expect core inflation to remain in the upper part of the
RBNZ’s target band through 2027.
Reinforcing the case for an OCR hike at the October meeting,
the New Zealand dollar is notably weaker than the RBNZ

assumed: on a trade-weighted basis, the NZD is currently
3.6% below the RBNZ’s September projections. If sustained,
that would add to inflation pressures over the next few
quarters. We suspect this will not have shifted the RBNZ’s
medium-term outlook for inflation.
Several other developments need to be weighed against that
firmer inflation outlook. First, wholesale long-term interest rates
have risen markedly, and there has also been a more moderate

rise in some mortgage rates. The RBNZ is likely to note the role of
those increases in managing medium-term inflation risks.
In addition, the RBNZ is likely to continue to emphasise that
there is little evidence of a lift in businesses’ longer-term plans
for price setting at this stage in response to the rise in energy
prices (sometimes referred to as “second-round inflation
effects”). We expect the RBNZ to highlight the risk that more
persistent inflation pressures could emerge, depending on the

duration of the oil price shock.
We also expect the RBNZ to note that the labour market
remains cyclically weak and is not a significant driver of inflation.
Employment growth that remains weak relative to labour force
growth, subdued growth in job vacancies and low labour market
turnover are consistent with this view and could be emphasised.
Finally, the RBNZ is likely to acknowledge that while the
global growth outlook remains solid, there are significant

downside risks from the Iran war and concerns about the
sustainability of the AI investment cycle.
Balancing those concerns, we think the MPC will lift the
cash rate next week. However, a vote seems very possible as
differences in view will likely remain among the MPC about
how much the medium-term inflation outlook has really
shifted in recent weeks.
In September, the bulk of the MPC saw upside risks to the
inflation outlook while two members (Conway and Hansen)

thought the risks were balanced. Recent developments will
not have made those seeing upside risks to inflation more
Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist

### Page 6

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
6
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
comfortable, while the more dovish MPC members will
likely focus on the ongoing downside risks for growth given
continued softness in house prices and the mounting pressure
on real incomes. Indeed, all MPC members saw some degree
of downside risks to growth which likely remain relevant. All

these messages should remain in the RBNZ’s updated views.
The MPC is likely to continue to present its decision as a
gradual evolution of the approach taken since July: a higher
OCR now provides insurance against the need for a much
higher OCR later if upside risks to medium-term inflation
materialise. We doubt the aim will be to push market interest
rates materially higher.
RBNZ to leave door open for further hikes, OCR
still expected to peak at 4% in 2027

In its forward guidance, the RBNZ is likely to leave open the option
of raising the OCR further at the December Monetary Policy
Statement, without presenting an increase as certain. While the
RBNZ will not publish an updated OCR projection at this review,
the press statement is likely to note that further increases may
be needed, depending on economic conditions. The September
projection showed the OCR eventually rising to 3.25%.
We expect the RBNZ will end up delivering another 25bp

hike in December, followed by a further hike in February.
That would take the OCR to 3.5%, roughly where the RBNZ
estimates the neutral rate to be. At that point, we expect the
RBNZ to slow the pace of rate increases (previously, we had
expected that point to be reached a little later).
We continue to expect further 25bp hikes at the June and
September 2027 meetings, taking the OCR to a peak of 4.0%.
While oil prices are generating significant swings in inflation,

underlying pressures remain firm. More restrictive conditions are
likely to be needed to bring core inflation sustainably back to 2%.
Scenarios
Our central scenario, with a probability of around 60%, is that
the RBNZ increases the OCR by 25bp and signals that a further
increase in December remains an option, without committing to
a markedly tighter future stance than it outlined in September.
Alternative scenarios include:
• A hawkish scenario (around a 20% probability) would see

the OCR increase to 3%, with the RBNZ placing greater
emphasis on upside risks to core and medium-term inflation.
That would suggest an upward revision to the RBNZ’s
projected OCR path in December. Downside risks to the
exchange rate could also be emphasised.
• A dovish scenario (around a 20% probability) would
see the RBNZ leave the OCR unchanged and signal that
the medium-term inflation outlook has not changed
meaningfully from its September forecast, despite higher

near-term inflation. In this scenario, the RBNZ would
reiterate its previous forecast: a 25bp increase in December
and one further hike during 2027.
How might the election play into the decision?
While next week’s meeting is close to the General Election
on 7 November, the decision taken by the MPC will not be
politically motivated. However, the election outcome is close.
And should it result in a Labour Party-led coalition, it seems
likely the new government would adopt the Labour Party’s

promise to restore the RBNZ’s dual mandate. That would
require the MPC to weigh the degree of labour market slack
more highly in its deliberations. As the degree of labour market
slack currently seems high, a new mandate might justify the
MPC taking more time to return inflation to the middle of the
target range (our earlier note discusses this issue). This could
figure into the deliberations of some MPC members if the
decision between leaving the OCR unchanged and raising it

now is viewed as finely balanced.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Sep Private Sector Credit (%mth)
Oct 30, Last: 0.6, Westpac f/c: 0.5
Market f/c: 0.5, Range: 0.4 to 0.6
Private sector credit growth came in slightly firmer than
expected at 0.6%mth in August, above Westpac’s and the
market’s forecast for a 0.5%mth gain, leaving credit growth

running at 8.4%yr. Growth in housing credit continued to ease,
as we expected, though the mix highlights that the waning in
momentum is uneven, centred on investors rather than owner-
occupiers. Business credit meanwhile remains a source of
strength, notwithstanding some slowing recently.
For September, we expect credit growth to nudge to 0.5%mth,
with the divergence between housing credit and business
credit persisting.
AUS: Q3/Sep CPI (%qtr/%mth)

Oct 28, Quarter, Last: 0.6%, Westpac f/c: 1.3%,
Market f/c: 1.3%, Range: 0.6 to 1.4.
Oct 28, Month, Last: 0.4%, Westpac f/c: 0.6%,
Market f/c: 0.6 Range: 0.3 to 0.8
As noted in the review of the August CPI, our September
quarter CPI estimate is unchanged at 1.3%qtr holding the
annual pace flat at 3.9%yr. At the margin there was a 0.04ppt
increase due to a lift in auto fuel but it still rounds to 1.3%.
The seasonally adjusted CPI gains 1.1% highlighting that part

of the increase is due to usual seasonality.
In the month, the September CPI is expected to rise 0.6% with
the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr. Recreation & culture make
the largest contribution but transport is not far behind. Our
seasonally adjusted estimate increases 0.5%.
For more details please see “ September CPI Preview “.
AUS: Q3/Sep Trimmed Mean (%qtr/mth)
Oct 28, Quarter, Last: 0.8%, Westpac f/c: 0.9%,
Market f/c: 1.0%, Range: 0.9 to 1.0.

Oct 28, Month, Last: 0.2%, Westpac f/c: 0.3%,
Market f/c: 0.3%, Range: 0.2 to 0.4
Our September quarter Trimmed Mean estimate is unchanged
at 0.9%qtr with the annual pace dipping a touch from 3.6%yr
to 3.5%yr. There was a small downward revision of 0.03ppt to
0.88% compared to our original published estimate but it still
rounds to 0.9%qtr.
In the month of September we are looking for a 0.3% in
the Trimmed Mean holding the annual pace steady at 3.6%

which will be the fourth consecutive month at this pace. The
distribution of the September Trimmed Mean ranges from
–0.3% to 0.7%.
For more details please see “ September CPI Preview “.

### Page 8

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.8
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
NZ: RBNZ Official Cash Rate Review (%)
Oct 28, Last: 2.75, Westpac f/c: 3.00, Mkt f/c: 3.00
We expect the RBNZ to raise the OCR by 25bps to 3% at
the 28 October Review. Data since September show a higher
outlook for year-end inflation and a stronger GDP starting

point, but increased risks to domestic consumption and
residential investment. On balance, the medium-term inflation
picture will probably be judged to have worsened slightly
given recent movements in energy prices and the exchange
rate. A vote seems very possible as differences in view will
likely remain among the MPC about how much the medium-
term inflation outlook has really shifted in recent weeks.
Forward guidance will likely leave open the option to raise

the OCR further at the December Monetary Policy Statement
without presenting it as a sure thing.
NZ: Oct ANZ Business Confidence (index)
Oct 29, Last: 51.9
Firms remained optimistic in the September survey of business
confidence, with both the activity and inflation measures
broadly holding steady. However, it’s likely that most responses
were submitted in the early part of the month, while fuel prices
took a sharp step higher from the second half of September. The

survey comes too late for the RBNZ’s October policy review, but
evidence about the breadth of cost and price pressures could
have a bearing on the RBNZ’s thinking about the total amount of
policy tightening needed in the months ahead.
NZ: Sep Monthly Employment Indicator (%mth)
Oct 28, Last: 0.2, Westpac f/c: 0.3
The MEI has been gradually ticking higher in recent months,
showing that the jobs market has held its ground through
the Iran conflict and the consequent surge in fuel prices. The

weekly snapshots provided by Stats NZ have been running
ahead of year-ago levels and suggest another solid result for
September – we’ve pencilled in a 0.3% increase. Given the
history of this measure, however, both this month and the
previous month are likely to be revised down a little from their
initial release.

### Page 9

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.9
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Oct FOMC meeting
Oct 27-28, Last: 3.875%, Mkt f/c: 3.875%, WBC f/c: 3.875%
Core CPI inflation and producer price inflation both surprised
to the upside in September, and the price of Brent oil has
risen back to around USD110. Underlying momentum in
the economy also remains robust, although employment

disappointed in the latest update. Given the concerns shown
by Committee members over price risks and their collective
resolve to return annual inflation to target, the above data
could easily justify a follow-up hike at the October meeting.
That said, Committee members who have spoken recently
have shown patience with respect to assessing the economy
and positioing policy. The market has responded, effectively
pricing out the chance of an October hike in favour of a

December move, which is also our expectation.
US Q3 GDP
Oct 29, %ann’d, Last: 2.2%, Mkt f/c: 3.0%, WBC f/c: 3.1%
The US economy continues to grow at a pace well above trend.
This outperformance is almost entirely due to the strength of
investment in data centres and other AI related assets. The
pipeline for this activity remains strong, limiting near-term
risks to the downside.
Abstracting from AI investment’s contribution, other business
investment and residential investment is weak, weighed down

by high term interest rates and uncertainty related to trade
policy. Household consumption growth is volatile month-to-
month, but the trend is solid. Absent a marked deterioration
in the labour market, which is not our expectation, this
momentum is likely to persist, as wealth continues to grow and
household income gains neutralises inflation. Come 2027 and
2028, growth is expected to slow to trend, but not below.

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco (AST)

Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD tăng trưởng tích cực trong 
2Q2026

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu

Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng 
trưởng doanh thu mới

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ cải thiện biên lợi nhuận

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá mục 
tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), với 
động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác 
tăng bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế 
gia tăng được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 
182 điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp từ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất và 6 điểm mới tại 
T2 Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Quốc và Gia Bình tiếp tục mở 
ra dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, 
phòng chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh được kỳ vọng 
tác động tích cực lên biên lợi nhuận của AST. Chúng tôi dự phóng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp đạt 64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 
48%/17% yoy.

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với AST, giá mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 21/09/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco là một 
trong những doanh nghiệp có quy mô mạng 
lưới điểm kinh doanh lớn trong lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ phi hàng không tại Việt Nam, với 141 
điểm kinh doanh tại 6 sân bay quốc tế tính 
đến cuối tháng 6/2026. Hoạt động cốt lõi của 
công ty bao gồm bán lẻ bách hóa lưu niệm 
(BHLN), hàng miễn thuế, dịch vụ ăn uống và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Ngoài ra, AST còn 
tham gia lĩnh vực suất ăn hàng không thông 
qua khoản đầu tư tại công ty liên kết VinaCS. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

2Q2026

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), 
với động lực: (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ 
VND (+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Biểu đồ 5. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh của AST

Nguồn:AST

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng tăng trưởng lưu lượng hành khách, đặc biệt là sự gia tăng tỷ 
trọng khách quốc tế, sẽ thúc đẩy doanh thu và hiệu quả kinh doanh của AST 
trong giai đoạn 2026–2030. Theo Cục Hàng không Việt Nam, lượng khách thông 
qua các cảng hàng không trong 7T2026 đạt hơn 76 triệu lượt (+7% yoy), trong 
đó khách quốc tế đạt gần 30 triệu lượt (+11% yoy), trong khi khách nội địa ghi 
nhận tăng trưởng 4% yoy. Xu hướng này được hỗ trợ bởi nhu cầu du lịch quốc 
tế, chính sách thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng bay, trong khi tăng 
trưởng kinh tế và thu nhập tiếp tục tạo nền tảng cho nhu cầu đi lại trong nước. 
Trên cơ sở đó, chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế và nội địa tại các thị 
trường AST khai thác tăng bình quân lần lượt 11% và 5%/năm đến 2030, qua đó 
nâng dần tỷ trọng khách quốc tế trong tổng lưu lượng, thúc đẩy doanh 
thu/điểm kinh doanh nhờ: (1) lưu lượng hành khách qua cảng hàng không gia 
tăng và (2) tỷ trọng khách quốc tế cao hơn hỗ trợ doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

 Biểu đồ 7. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh AST (tỷ VND)

Nguồn: AST  Nguồn: AST

Mở rộng mạng lưới tại các nhà ga 
hiện hữu và sân bay mới, gia tăng 
quy mô kinh doanh đến 2030 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng chu kỳ mở rộng hạ tầng hàng không giai đoạn 2026–2030 sẽ 
tạo dư địa gia tăng quy mô kinh doanh cho AST, với số điểm kinh doanh dự 
phóng đạt 145 điểm vào cuối 2026 và tăng lên 182 điểm vào năm 2030. Cụ thể: 
- Trong ngắn hạn, tăng trưởng năm 2026 được hỗ trợ bởi việc ghi nhận 
đủ năm doanh thu từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
trong năm 2025, cùng 6 điểm mới tại T2 Nội Bài trong năm 2026, trong 
đó có 2 cửa hàng miễn thuế và 2 cửa hàng Memories of Vietnam khai 
trương trong 2Q2026. 
- Trong trung và dài hạn, các dự án Cảng HKQT Long Thành, Cảng HKQT 
Phú Quốc nâng cấp mở rộng và Cảng HKQT Gia Bình sẽ góp phần thúc 
đẩy ngành hàng không Việt Nam nói chung, bổ sung mặt bằng thương 
mại, mở ra cơ hội phát triển các mảng kinh doanh cốt lõi cho AST nói 
riêng bao gồm các mảng bán lẻ, miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Với kinh nghiệm khai thác 141 điểm kinh doanh 
tại 6 sân bay quốc tế và lịch sử trúng thầu tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất, T2 Nội 
Bài trong thời gian qua, chúng tôi kỳ vọng AST có khả năng cạnh tranh 
cao trong việc đấu thầu quyền khai thác mặt bằng tại các sân bay mới, 
đặc biệt là Gia Bình, qua đó duy trì vị thế và thị phần kinh doanh phi 
hàng không tại khu vực phía Bắc khi một phần lưu lượng hành khách 
được phân bổ lại từ Nội Bài.

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ mở rộng biên lợi nhuận  
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng biên lợi nhuận của AST tiếp tục cải thiện trong 2026 - 2027, 
nhờ định hướng mở rộng các mảng có biên lợi nhuận tốt như hàng miễn thuế 
và phòng chờ thương gia, kết hợp tối ưu hiệu quả vận hành tại các điểm kinh 
doanh. Cụ thể: (1) AST ưu tiên gia tăng số điểm kinh doanh hàng miễn thuế 
nhằm đón đầu tăng trưởng nhanh và ổn định của lượng khách quốc tế, qua đó 
nâng đóng góp lợi nhuận từ hoạt động mua sắm tại sân bay, (2) mở rộng mạng 
lưới phòng chờ, đồng thời gia tăng lượng khách sử dụng trên nền năng lực phục 
vụ hiện hữu và (3) tối ưu hóa hiệu quả hoạt động thông qua cải thiện bố trí mặt 
bằng, danh mục sản phẩm và kiểm soát chi phí tại các điểm kinh doanh, qua 
đó tăng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thu được trên mỗi khách hàng.

Biên LNST 14.49% 16.95% +246 bps 18.54% +159 bps

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá 
mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu AST, với giá mục tiêu 76,500 
VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 
21/09/2026, dựa trên triển vọng tăng trưởng từ lượng khách quốc tế, mở rộng 
mạng lưới điểm kinh doanh và khả năng cải thiện biên lợi nhuận của doanh 
nghiệp. Chúng tôi sử dụng phương pháp định giá từng phần (SOTP), bao gồm 
DCF cho hoạt động kinh doanh cốt lõi của AST và P/E mục tiêu 11x đối với phần 
lợi nhuận thuộc sở hữu AST tại VinaCS. Các giả định chính gồm: (1) lượng khách 
quốc tế/nội địa tăng trung bình 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030, (2) số điểm kinh 
doanh tăng từ 145 điểm cuối 2026 lên 182 điểm năm 2030, với giả định AST 
trúng thầu mặt bằng tại cả Long Thành và Gia Bình và (3) biên lợi nhuận gộp 
2026/2027 đạt 64.7/65.5%. Đối với VinaCS, chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhà máy suất ăn 
Long Thành sẽ hỗ trợ tăng trưởng lợi nhuận trong trung và dài hạn khi dự kiến 
có thể bắt đầu đóng góp lợi nhuận cho AST từ 2028.

Rủi ro đối với định giá bao gồm: (1) AST không giành được quyền khai thác tại 
Long Thành và Gia Bình như kỳ vọng, (2) lưu lượng khách dịch chuyển giữa các 
sân bay mới và hiện hữu khiến doanh thu tại một số điểm kinh doanh bị sụt 
giảm, (3) cạnh tranh gia tăng từ các doanh nghiệp kinh doanh dịch vụ phi hàng 
không khác và (4) căng thẳng địa chính trị kéo dài, giá dầu tăng gây áp lực lên 
hoạt động hàng không và chi tiêu của hành khách.

## Document 271: CTCP TCT Nước - Môi trường Bình Dương - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP TCT Nước - Môi trường Bình Dương - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-23).pdf

### Page 1

Doanh thu 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ do 
không còn ghi nhận doanh thu 1 lần 
từ phí nước thải; trong khi LNST 
công ty mẹ tăng trưởng 1 chữ số

Tiêu thụ nước khu vực dân sinh được 
thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng nóng 
hơn; trong khi triển vọng nhóm 
khách hàng công nghiệp bị ảnh 
hưởng bởi bất lợi thuế quan

Đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyện và xử 
lý rác thải được phê duyệt cho phép 
cải thiện doanh thu và lợi nhuận của 
mảng này

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
59,000 VNĐ/cổ phiếu

Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 325 tỷ 
VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ), ghi nhận tăng trưởng của tất cả các mảng. Mảng cung cấp 
nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 
785 tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An vận hành từ Q3/2025 
giúp sản lượng nước tăng 28% YoY.

Cường độ và thời gian kéo dài của các đợt nắng nóng trong 2026/2027 là cơ sở để 
chúng tôi điều chỉnh tăng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước của nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phước trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY. Trái lại, 
triển vọng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm sản xuất ảm đạm do tình hình thuế 
quan có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho các ngành xuất khẩu truyền thống 
của Việt Nam.

UBND TP.HCM công bố đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyển và xử lý rác thải mới, tăng 
mạnh từ 50-150% nhằm phản ánh đầy đủ và chính xác hơn các chi phí phát sinh 
trong thực tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng điều này sẽ tác động tích cực với BWE khi các dự 
án của doanh nghiệp ở mảng này sẽ vận hành hiệu quả hơn. Dự báo doanh thu và 
lợi nhuận gộp của mảng xử lý rác thải lần lượt đạt 1,069/1,234 và 165.7/180.7 tỷ 
VND lần lượt trong 2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% và 27%/9% YoY.

Kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá FCFF và EV/EBITDA, KBSV khuyến nghị MUA với 
BWE, giá mục tiêu 59,000 VND, tương đương với mức lợi suất kỳ vọng 42.2% so với 
giá đóng cửa ngày 22/09/2026.
